Weightlist
Weightlists
Whether this material will animate morphs (flexes) when it is applied to a model that has them. If this is false the model still animates, its morphs just do nothing. On a model that is meant to morph the usual cause is the material's Morph feature (F_MORPH_SUPPORTED) being turned off, so turn it on in the material. This looks for any of the shader's morph paths: a MorphIndex vertex input, D_MORPH, or D_CS_VERTEX_ANIMATION (a compute pre-pass, which morphs as well as skins).
Whether this material will animate when it is applied to a model with bones. If this is false the mesh renders in bind pose, whatever the animation does. Shaders written for environments, decals and effects often don't support skinning, so if a model needs to animate, use a shader meant for models. This looks for any of the shader's skinning paths: a BlendIndices vertex input, D_BLEND_WEIGHT_COUNT (skinning in the vertex shader) or D_CS_VERTEX_ANIMATION (skinning in a compute pre-pass).
Whether to draw a 3d debug visualization of analysis (debug draw persists until next invocation of this wizard)
Which set of texture coordinates to display.
